COMPANY PROFILE



 華潤萬象生活有限公司（以下簡稱：華潤萬象
 生 活，股 票 代 碼 1209.HK）是 世 界 500 強 企 業
 華潤（集團）有限公司（「華潤集團」旗下戰略業                  China Resources Mixc Lifestyle Services Limited (“CR Mixc
 務單元華潤置地有限公司（「華潤置地」）的成員                  Lifestyle”)(stock code: 1209.HK) is a member of China
 公司。憑藉卓越的全業態運營能力、廣泛的平                    Resources Land Limited (“CR Land”), a strategic business unit
 台資源及強大的會員體系，以「城市品質生活                    of China Resources (Holdings) Company Limited (“CR Group”),
 服務平台」為定位，攜手華潤集團、華潤置地                    a Fortune Global 500 company. Capitalizing on our superior
 及其他合作夥伴，圍繞「空間、客戶、服務」                    operational capabilities in multiple property types, extensive
 構建全業態、全客戶、全產品、全服務一體化                    platform resources and powerful membership programs,
 生態體系。                                   positioned as “urban high-quality lifestyle service platform”,
                                         together with CR Group, CR Land and other partners, we are
 截至二零二零年十二月三十一日，華潤萬象生                    committed to create an integrated ecosystem that is centered
 活提供物業管理服務的住宅及商業物業建築面                    around “space, customers and service”, and covers multiple
 積已達約 1.186 億平方米，提供商業運營服務                property types, all customers and full spectrum of service

主席報告
CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT


 二零二零年十二月九日，華潤萬象生活有限公司                    On 9 December 2020, China Resources Mixc Lifestyle Services Limited
（「本公司」，連同其附屬公司統稱「本集團」）於香                  (the “Company”, and together with its subsidiaries referred to as the
 港聯合交易所有限公司（「聯交所」）主板成功上市，                 “Group”) was successfully listed on the Main Board of The Stock
 成為當年聯交所第三大首次公開發售（「首次公開                   Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock Exchange”), being the
 發售」）。上市以來，本集團市盈率保持行業領先，                  third largest initial public offering (the “IPO”) on the Stock Exchange
 並於二零二一年二月二十六日晉升為恒生綜合指                    in the year. The Group has maintained a leading P/E ratio in the
 數成份股和恒生滬港通大灣區綜合指數成份股。                    industry since its listing, and was included as a constituent of Hang
 在此，本人欣然向各位股東提呈本集團截至二零                    Seng Composite Index and Hang Seng Shanghai-Hong Kong Stock
 二零年十二月三十一日止年度的年度業務回顧與                    Connect Greater Bay Area Composite Index on 26 February 2021. I
 展望。                                      am pleased to present the annual business review and outlook of the
                                          Group for the year ended 31 December 2020 to the shareholders.

二零二零年，面對突如其來的新冠疫情，舉國上                     2020 was an extraordinary year. The outbreak of COVID-19 drew
下每一個平凡人將自己的涓滴之力匯聚成磅礡偉                     every effort together to fight the battle, and we won. China was the
力，眾志成城打贏疫情防控狙擊戰，共同書寫了                     only major economy in the world recording a positive economic
歷史上極不平凡的一卷。中國成為全球唯一實現                     growth, with GDP hitting RMB100 trillion for the first time, a satisfied
經 濟 正 增 長 的 主 要 經 濟 體，全 年 經 濟 總 量 邁 上     performance for the last year of the “13th Five-Year Plan”. During the
100 萬億元新台階，為「十三五」收官畫上圓滿句                  past five years, the real estate industry in China has gradually shifted
號。過去五年，隨著中國房地產行業價值鏈重心                     its focus in the industrial value chain from development to operation.
由前端開發逐漸向後端運營轉移，伴隨城鎮化進                     Along with the progress in urbanization and city development as well
程和城市發展、消費升級和技術迭代的潮流，中                     as upgrade of consumption and technology, China’s property
國物業管理及商業運營服務市場快速增長。                       management and commercial operation service market experienced
                                          rapid growth.

二零二零年，本集團全面落實「抗疫情、保業績」                    In 2020, the Group arranged its works under the principle of “fighting
和「降本、提質、增效」兩大管理主題。一手抓「防                   against epidemic and ensuring to achieve performance targets” and
疫抗疫」，一手抓「復工復產」，各項核心業績指標                   implemented management measures of “cost reduction, service
穩健增長。全年實現綜合收入人民幣 67.8 億元，                 quality improvement and efficiency enhancement”. The Group
按 年 增 長 15.5%，實 現 淨 利 潤 人 民 幣 817.7 百 萬   focused on both “fighting epidemic” and “production resumption”
元，按年增長 124.1%。實現每股股東應佔盈利人                 to realize stable growth in each core performance target. In 2020, the
民幣 0.485 元，其中每股核心股東應佔溢利人民幣                Group realized a consolidated revenue of RMB6.78 billion, an increase
0.484 元。本公司董事會（「董事會」）決議宣派末期               of 15.5% YoY and a net profit of RMB817.7 million, an increase of
股息每股人民幣0.132元（折合每股港幣0.158元），              124.1% YoY. The Group’s earnings per share was RMB0.485, and
全年派息率為 37%。                               core profit per share was RMB0.484. The board of directors (the
                                          “Board”) of the Company has resolved to declare a final dividend of
                                          RMB0.132 (equivalent to HK$0.158) per share, with an annual
                                          distribution rate of 37%.

財務回顧                                          FINANCIAL REVIEW

收益                                            REVENUE

本集團收入主要來自兩大業務板塊：(i) 住宅物業                      The Group’s revenue is mainly generated from two business
管理服務及 (ii) 商業運營及物業管理服務。                       segments: (i) residential property management services and (ii)
                                              commercial operational and property management services.

截至二零二零年十二月三十一日止年度內，本集                         During the year ended 31 December 2020, the Group’s revenue
團收入為人民幣 6,778.9 百萬元，較去年同期增長                   amounted to RMB6,778.9 million, representing an increase of 15.5%

風險管理及內部監控                              RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROLS

董事會負責本公司的風險管理及內部監控系統及                  The Board is responsible for the Company’s risk management and
持續檢討其成效。本集團內部審核團隊及高級管                  internal control systems and for reviewing its effectiveness on an
理層則檢討本集團風險管理及內部監控系統的成                  ongoing basis. The Group’s internal audit team and senior
效。審核委員會每年至少開會兩次，以檢討內部                  management conduct reviews of the effectiveness of the risk
審核團隊及高級管理層的調查結果及推薦建議，                  management and internal control systems of the Group. The Audit
並向董事會匯報有關檢討結果。                         Committee reviews the findings and recommendations of the internal
                                       audit team and the senior management in their meetings at least
                                       twice a year and reports to the Board on such review.

風險管理及內部監控系統的設計旨在管理（而非消                 The risk management and internal control systems are designed to
除）業務風險、防止本集團資產受到欺詐或其他違                 manage, rather than eliminate business risk; to help safeguard the
規情況的損害，以及提供合理（但非絕對）保證以                 Group’s assets against fraud and other irregularities; and to give

退休福利計劃                         RETIREMENT BENEFITS SCHEME

中國附屬公司的僱員均為中國政府經營的國家管          The employees of the PRC subsidiaries are members of the state-
理退休福利計劃成員。中國附屬公司的僱員須按          managed retirement benefits scheme operated by the PRC
彼等薪金的若干百分比向退休福利計劃供款。本          government. The employees of the PRC subsidiaries are required to
集團於該退休福利計劃之唯一責任為作出計劃規          contribute a certain percentage of their payroll to the retirement
定的供款。                          benefits scheme to fund the benefits. The only obligation of the
                               Group with respect to this retirement benefits scheme is to make the
                               required contributions under the scheme.

意見                                      OPINION

本核數師（以下簡稱「我們」）已審核列載於第 110               We have audited the consolidated financial statements of China
頁至第 223 頁華潤萬象生活有限公司（「貴公司」）              Resources Mixc Lifestyle Services Limited (the “Company”) and its
及其附屬公司（以下統稱「貴集團」）的綜合財務報                 subsidiaries (the “Group”) set out on pages 110 to 223, which
表，此 綜 合 財 務 報 表 包 括 於 二 零 二 零 年 十 二 月   comprise the consolidated statement of financial position as at 31
三十一日的綜合財務狀況表，以及截至該日止年                   December 2020, and the consolidated statement of profit or loss and
度的綜合損益及其他全面收益表、綜合權益變動                   other comprehensive income, the consolidated statement of changes
表及綜合現金流量表，以及綜合財務報表附註，                   in equity and the consolidated statement of cash flows for the year
包括主要會計政策概要。                             then ended, and notes to the consolidated financial statements,
                                        including a summary of significant accounting policies.

我們認為，該等綜合財務報表已根據香港會計師                   In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a true and
公會（「香港會計師公會」）頒佈的香港財務報告準                 fair view of the consolidated financial position of the Group as at 31
則（「香港財務報告準則」）真實而中肯地反映 貴                 December 2020, and of its consolidated financial performance and its
集團於二零二零年十二月三十一日的綜合財務狀                   consolidated cash flows for the year then ended in accordance with
況以及截至該日止年度的綜合財務表現及綜合現                   Hong Kong Financial Reporting Standards (“HKFRSs”) issued by the
金流量，並已遵照香港公司條例的披露規定妥為                   Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public Accountants (“HKICPA”) and
編製。                                     have been properly prepared in compliance with the disclosure
                                        requirements of the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.

意見的基準                                   BASIS FOR OPINION

 我們已根據香港會計師公會頒佈的香港審計準則                  We conducted our audit in accordance with Hong Kong Standards on
（「香港審計準則」）進行審核。我們於該等準則項                 Auditing (“HKSAs”) issued by the HKICPA. Our responsibilities under
 下的責任已於本報告「核數師就審核綜合財務報                  those standards are further described in the Auditor’s responsibilities
 表須承擔的責任 」一節中作進一步闡述。根據香港                for the audit of the consolidated financial statements section of our
 會計師公會頒佈的「專業會計師職業道德守則 」
                      （「守               report. We are independent of the Group in accordance with the
 則」），我們獨立於 貴集團，並已履行守則中的                 HKICPA’s Code of Ethics for Professional Accountants (the “Code”),
 其他專業道德責任。我們相信，我們所獲得的審                  and we have fulfilled our other ethical responsibilities in accordance
 核憑證能充足及適當地為我們的審核意見提供基                  with the Code. We believe that the audit evidence we have obtained
 準。                                     is sufficient and appropriate to provide a basis for our opinion.

INDEPENDENT AUDITOR’S REPORT


關鍵審核事項                                            KEY AUDIT MATTERS

關鍵審核事項是根據我們的專業判斷，認為對本                             Key audit matters are those matters that, in our professional
期間綜合財務報表的審核最為重要的事項。該等                             judgement, were of most significance in our audit of the consolidated
事項於我們審核整體綜合財務報表及出具意見時                             financial statements of the current period. These matters were
進行處理，而我們不會對該等事項提供單獨的意                             addressed in the context of our audit of the consolidated financial
見。就下文各事項而言，我們的審計如何處理關                             statements as a whole, and in forming our opinion thereon, and we

        的定義作出澄清，並提供額外指引。                         guidance on the definition of a business. The amendments
        該等修訂說明，就被視為業務之一組                         clarify that for an integrated set of activities and assets to
        活動及資產組合而言，其必須至少包                         be considered a business, it must include, at a minimum,
        含可共同對創造產出的能力做出重大                         an input and a substantive process that together
        貢獻的輸入資源及實質性過程。業務                         significantly contribute to the ability to create output. A
        可以不包括創造產出所需之所有投入                         business can exist without including all of the inputs and
        及過程而存在。該等修訂取消對市場                         processes needed to create outputs. The amendments
        參與者是否有能力收購業務並繼續創                         remove the assessment of whether market participants
        造產出之評估，轉為重點關注獲得之                         are capable of acquiring the business and continue to
        投入及實質性過程是否共同對創造產                         produce outputs. Instead, the focus is on whether
        出能力有顯著貢獻。該等修訂亦已收                         acquired inputs and acquired substantive processes
        窄產出的定義範圍，重點關注向客戶                         together significantly contribute to the ability to create
        提供之貨品或服務、投資收入或日常                         outputs. The amendments have also narrowed the
        活動產生的其他收入。此外，該等修                         definition of outputs to focus on goods or services
        訂提供指引以評估收購過程是否具有                         provided to customers, investment income or other
        實質性，並引入公允價值集中度測試                         income from ordinary activities. Furthermore, the
        選項，允許對所獲得的一組活動及資                         amendments provide guidance to assess whether an
        產是否不屬於業務進行簡化評估。本                         acquired process is substantive and introduce an optional
        集團已將該等修訂預期應用於二零二                         fair value concentration test to permit a simplified

        他事件。該等修訂並無對本集團的財                         assets is not a business. The Group has applied the

  (e)   香港會計準則第 1 號及香港會計準則                 (e)   Amendments to HKAS 1 and HKAS 8 provide a new
        第 8 號修訂本為重大提供一個新定義。                      definition of material. The new definition states that
        新定義訂明，倘資料之遺漏、錯誤陳                         information is material if omitting, misstating or obscuring
        述或模糊不清可合理預期影響一般用                         it could reasonably be expected to influence decisions that
        途財務報表之主要使用者按該等財務                         the primary users of general purpose financial statements
        報表作出之決定，則資料屬重大。該                         make on the basis of those financial statements. The
        等修訂本釐清，重要性將視乎資料之                         amendments clarify that materiality will depend on the
        性質或量級或兼顧兩者而定。該等修                         nature or magnitude of information, or both. The
        訂本並無對本集團財務狀況及表現造                         amendments did not have any significant impact on the
        成任何重大影響。                                 financial position and performance of the Group.

  於合營企業投資                                  INVESTMENTS IN JOINT VENTURES

  合營企業為一項合營安排，據此擁有安排共                      A joint venture is a type of joint arrangement whereby the
  同控制權的訂約方有權享有合營安企業排的                      parties that have joint control of the arrangement have rights to
  資產淨值。共同控制權指按照合同協定對一                      the net assets of the joint venture. Joint control is the
  項安排所共有的控制權，僅在相關活動必須                      contractually agreed sharing of control of an arrangement,
  獲得共同享有控制權的各方一致同意方能決                      which exists only when decisions about the relevant activities
  定時存在。                                    require the unanimous consent of the parties sharing control.

  本集團於合營企業的投資在綜合財務狀況表                      The Group's investments in joint ventures are stated in the
  中，以按照權益法計算的本集團所佔淨資產                      consolidated statement of financial position at the Group's
  份額減去任何減值損失後的餘額列示。會計                      share of net assets under the equity method of accounting, less
  政策存在不一致的，應當對其進行調整。                       any impairment losses. Adjustments are made to bring into line
                                           any dissimilar accounting policies that may exist.

  合收益的份額分別計入綜合損益表及其他全                      comprehensive income of joint ventures is included in the
  面收益表。另外，當合營企業的權益發生變                      consolidated statement of profit or loss and other

  確認變化的相應份額。本集團與合營企業之                      change recognised directly in the equity of the joint venture, the
  間因交易產生的未實現收益或損失，在本集                      Group recognises its share of any changes, when applicable, in
  團於合營企業中的投資的範圍內予以抵銷，                      the consolidated statement of changes in equity. Unrealised
  除非有證據表明未實現的損失屬於所轉讓資                      gains and losses resulting from transactions between the Group
  產發生減值損失。本集團對合營企業的投資                      and its joint ventures are eliminated to the extent of the Group’s

  公允價值計量                   FAIR VALUE MEASUREMENT

 本集團於各報告期末按公允價值計量其投資       The Group measures its investment properties and financial
 物業及以公允價值計量且其變動計入當期收       assets at fair value through profit or loss at fair value at the end
 益的金融資產。公允價值指於計量日期市場       of each reporting period. Fair value is the price that would be
 參與者之間的有序交易中，就出售資產所收       received to sell an asset or paid to transfer a liability in an
 取的價格或轉讓負債所支付的價格。公允價       orderly transaction between market participants at the
 值計量乃基於假設出售資產或轉讓負債的交       measurement date. The fair value measurement is based on the
 易於資產或負債的主要市場，或在未有主要       presumption that the transaction to sell the asset or transfer the
 市場的情況下，則於資產或負債最有利市場       liability takes place either in the principal market for the asset or
 進行。主要或最有利市場須位於本集團能到       liability, or in the absence of a principal market, in the most
 達的地方。資產或負債的公允價值乃使用市       advantageous market for the asset or liability. The principal or
 場參與者為資產或負債定價所用的假設計量       the most advantageous market must be accessible by the
 （假設市場參與者依照彼等最佳經濟利益行       Group. The fair value of an asset or a liability is measured using
 事）。                       the assumptions that market participants would use when
                           pricing the asset or liability, assuming that market participants
                           act in their economic best interest.

  非金融資產公允價值之計量則參考市場參與      A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into
  者可從使用該資產得到之最高及最佳效用，      account a market participant’s ability to generate economic
  或把該資產售予另一可從使用該資產得到最      benefits by using the asset in its highest and best use or by
  高及最佳效用之市場參與者所產生之經濟效      selling it to another market participant that would use the asset
  益。                       in its highest and best use.

  折舊按直線法計算，以將每項物業、廠房及             Depreciation is calculated on the straight-line basis to write off
  設備的成本於其估計可使用年期內撇減至其             the cost of each item of property, plant and equipment to its

  初次確認及計量（續）                             Initial recognition and measurement (continued)

  本集團管理金融資產的業務模式反映其管理                    The Group’s business model for managing financial assets refers
  金融資產，以產生現金流量的方式。業務模                    to how it manages its financial assets in order to generate cash
  式決定現金流量將因獲取合同現金流量、出                    flows. The business model determines whether cash flows will
  售金融資產或兩者同時產生。按攤銷成本分                    result from collecting contractual cash flows, selling the financial
  類及計量的金融資產按目標為持有金融資產                    assets, or both. Financial assets classified and measured at
  以獲取合同現金流量的業務模式持有，而按                    amortised cost are held within a business model with the
  公允價值計量且其變動計入其他全面收入分                    objective to hold financial assets in order to collect contractual
  類及計量的金融資產按目標為同時獲取合同                    cash flows, while financial assets classified and measured at fair
  現金流量及出售的業務模式持有。並非按上                    value through other comprehensive income are held within a
  述業務模式持有的金融資產按公允價值計量                    business model with the objective of both holding to collect
  且其變動計入當期損益分類及計量。                       contractual cash flows and selling. Financial assets which are
                                         not held within the aforementioned business models are
                                         classified and measured at fair value through profit or loss.

  所有金融資產的常規買賣於交易日期（即本                    All regular way purchases and sales of financial assets are
  集團承諾購買或出售資產之日）確認。常規                    recognised on the trade date, that is, the date that the Group
  買賣指需要於市場規例或慣例一般規定的期                    commits to purchase or sell the asset. Regular way purchases or
  限內交付資產的金融資產買賣。                         sales are purchases or sales of financial assets that require
                                         delivery of assets within the period generally established by
                                         regulation or convention in the marketplace.

  後續計量                                   Subsequent measurement

  金融資產的後續計量視乎其分類如下：                      The subsequent measurement of financial assets depends on
                                         their classification as follows:

  按攤銷成本計量的金融資產（債務工具）                     Financial assets at amortised cost (debt instruments)

  按攤銷成本計量的金融資產隨後使用實際利                    Financial assets at amortised cost are subsequently measured
  率法計量，並可予減值。在資產終止確認、                    using the effective interest method and are subject to
  修訂或減值時，收益及虧損於損益表確認。                    impairment. Gains and losses are recognised in the statement of
                                         profit or loss when the asset is derecognised, modified or
                                         impaired.

  按公允價值計量且其變動計入當期損益的金                    Financial assets at fair value through profit or loss
  融資產

  按公允價值計量且其變動計入當期損益的金                    Financial assets at fair value through profit or loss are carried in
  融資產乃按於損益表內確認之公允價值之變                    the statement of financial position at fair value with net changes
  動淨額於財務狀況表列賬。                           in fair value recognised in the statement of profit or loss.

      控制權。                            rewards of the asset, but has transferred control of the

2.4 關鍵會計政策概要（續）                       2.4 SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING

  金融資產減值                                 IMPAIRMENT OF FINANCIAL ASSETS

  本集團就所有並非按公允價值計量且其變動                    The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit losses
  計入當期損益而持有的債務工具確認預期信                    (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at fair value through
  貸虧損（「預期信貸虧損」）撥備。預期信貸虧                  profit or loss. ECLs are based on the difference between the
  損基於按照合同到期的合同現金流量與本集                    contractual cash flows due in accordance with the contract and
  團預計收到的所有現金流量之間的差額，按                    all the cash flows that the Group expects to receive, discounted
  接近原始實際利率的利率貼現。預期現金流                    at an approximation of the original effective interest rate. The
  量將包括出售所持抵押品或為合同條款一部                    expected cash flows will include cash flows from the sale of
  分的其他信用增強措施的現金流量。                       collateral held or other credit enhancements that are integral to
                                         the contractual terms.

  一般法                                    General approach

   預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。就自初始確                   ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures for
   認起信貸風險並未大幅增加的信貸風險而                    which there has not been a significant increase in credit risk
   言，預期信貸虧損就可能於未來 12 個月內                 since initial recognition, ECLs are provided for credit losses that
   發生的違約事件導致的信貸虧損作出撥備                    result from default events that are possible within the next 12
  （12 個月預期信貸虧損）。就自初始確認起信                 months (a 12-month ECL). For those credit exposures for which
   貸風險已大幅增加的信貸風險而言，須於風                   there has been a significant increase in credit risk since initial
   險的餘下年期內就信貸虧損作出虧損撥備（不                  recognition, a loss allowance is required for credit losses
   論違約時間）（整個存續期預期信貸虧損）。                  expected over the remaining life of the exposure, irrespective of
                                         the timing of the default (a lifetime ECL).

  於各報告日期，本集團評估金融工具的信貸                    At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether the credit
  風險自初始確認起是否已大幅增加。在作出                    risk on a financial instrument has increased significantly since
  評估時，本集團將金融工具於報告日期發生                    initial recognition. When making the assessment, the Group
  違約的風險與金融工具於初始確認日期發生                    compares the risk of a default occurring on the financial
  違約的風險進行比較，考慮無需過多成本或                    instrument as at the reporting date with the risk of a default
  努力即可獲得的合理有支持資料（包括歷史                    occurring on the financial instrument as at the date of initial
  及前瞻性資料）。                               recognition and considers reasonable and supportable
                                         information that is available without undue cost or effort,
                                         including historical and forward-looking information.

  當合同付款逾期 90 天時，本集團認為金融                  The Group considers a financial asset in default when
  資產已違約。然而，在若干情況下，當內部                    contractual payments are 90 days past due. However, in certain
  或外部資料表明本集團不大可能收回全部未                    cases, the Group may also consider a financial asset to be in
  收回的合同款項（在考慮本集團持有的任何                    default when internal or external information indicates that the
  信貸增強措施前）時，本集團亦可能將金融                    Group is unlikely to receive the outstanding contractual
  資產視為違約。當合理預期無法收回合同現                    amounts in full before taking into account any credit
  金流量時，金融資產予以撇銷。                         enhancements held by the Group. A financial asset is written off
                                         when there is no reasonable expectation of recovering the
                                         contractual cash flows.

  風險的變化，而是於各報告日期根據整個存         Under the simplified approach, the Group does not track

  撥備在現有義務（法定或推定）因過往事件而                   A provision is recognised when a present obligation (legal or
  產生且未來很可能需要流出資源以清償義務                    constructive) has arisen as a result of a past event and it is
  時確認，前提是義務金額能夠可靠估計。                     probable that a future outflow of resources will be required to
                                         settle the obligation, provided that a reliable estimate can be

  值。隨時間推移而產生的已貼現現值之增                     reporting period of the future expenditures expected to be
  加，計入損益表內的財務費用。                         required to settle the obligation. The increase in the discounted
                                         present value amount arising from the passage of time is

  的大綱及組織章程細則授予董事宣派中期股                    because the Company's memorandum and articles of

  派時即刻確認為負債。                             dividends. Consequently, interim dividends are recognised

  外幣                                     FOREIGN CURRENCIES

  該等財務報表以人民幣呈列，人民幣為本公                    These financial statements are presented in RMB, which is the
  司的功能貨幣。本集團各實體決定其自身的                    Company’s functional currency. Each entity in the Group
  功能貨幣，各實體財務資料中的項目使用該                    determines its own functional currency and items included in
  功能貨幣計量。外幣交易由本集團內實體使                    the financial information of each entity are measured using that
  用交易日期各自的現行功能貨幣匯率初始入                    functional currency. Foreign currency transactions recorded by
  賬。以外幣計值的貨幣資產及負債按報告期                    the entities in the Group are initially recorded using their
  末的現行功能貨幣匯率換算。因結算或換算                    respective functional currency rates prevailing at the dates of
  貨幣項目而產生的差額於損益確認。                       the transactions. Monetary assets and liabilities denominated in
                                         foreign currencies are translated at the functional currency rates
                                         of exchange ruling at the end of the reporting period.
                                         Differences arising on settlement or translation of monetary
                                         items are recognised in profit or loss.

     估計不確定因素（續）                          ESTIMATION UNCERTAINTY (continued)
     租賃 — 估計增量借款利率                       Leases — Estimating the incremental borrowing rate

     本集團無法輕易釐定租賃中隱含的利率，因                 The Group cannot readily determine the interest rate implicit in
     此，本集團使用增量借款利率（「增量借款利                a lease, and therefore, it uses an incremental borrowing rate
     率」）計量租賃負債。增量借款利率指本集團                (“IBR”) to measure lease liabilities. The IBR is the rate of interest
     在類似經濟環境下按類似期限、以類似抵押                 that the Group would have to pay to borrow over a similar
     品借入取得價值與使用權資產接近的資產所                 term, and with a similar security, the funds necessary to obtain
     需的資金而須支付的利率。因此，增量借款                 an asset of a similar value to the right-of-use asset in a similar
     利率反映本集團「須支付」的利率，當並無                 economic environment. The IBR therefore reflects what the
     可觀察利率時（如對並無訂立融資交易的附                 Group “would have to pay”, which requires estimation when
     屬公司而言），或需要調整利率以反映租賃                 no observable rates are available (such as for subsidiaries that
     條款及條件時（例如，當租賃並非以附屬公                 do not enter into financing transactions) or when it needs to be
     司的功能貨幣訂立時），需要估計該利率。                 adjusted to reflect the terms and conditions of the lease. (for
     當可觀察輸入數據（如市場利率）可用時，本                example, when leases are not in the subsidiary’s functional
     集團使用可觀察輸入數據估算遞增借貸利率                 currency). The Group estimates the IBR using observable inputs
     並須作出若干實體特定的估計（如附屬公司                 (such as market interest rates) when available and is required to
     單獨的信貸評級）。                           make certain entity-specific estimates (such as the subsidiary’s
                                         stand-alone credit rating).

18. 遞延稅項（續）                             18. DEFERRED TAX (continued)

  根據《中華人民共和國企業所得稅法》，於中                           Pursuant to the PRC Corporate Income Tax Law, a 10%
  國內地成立的外商投資企業向外國投資者宣                            withholding tax is levied on dividends declared to foreign
  派的股息須按 10% 的稅率繳納預扣稅。該                          investors from the foreign investment enterprises established in
  規定自二零零八年一月一日起生效，適用於                            Mainland China. The requirement is effective from 1 January
  二零零七年十二月三十一日後的盈利。倘中                            2008 and applies to earnings after 31 December 2007. A lower
  國內地與外國投資者所在司法管轄區訂有稅                            withholding tax rate may be applied if there is a tax treaty
  收協定，可適用較低的預扣稅稅率。對本集                            between Mainland China and the jurisdiction of the foreign
  團而言，適用稅率為 5%。因此，本集團須                           investors. For the Group, the applicable rate is 5%. The Group
  就於中國內地成立的附屬公司就二零零八年                            is therefore liable for withholding taxes on dividends distributed
  一月一日起產生的盈利所分派的股息繳納預                            by those subsidiaries established in Mainland China in respect of

  於各報告日期使用撥備矩陣進行減值分析，                             An impairment analysis is performed at each reporting date
  以計量預期信貸虧損。撥備率乃基於具有類                             using a provision matrix to measure ECLs. The provision rates
  似虧損模式的多個客戶分類組別（即客戶類                             are based on days past due for groupings of various customer
  型及服務類型）的逾期天數釐定。計算反映                             segments with similar loss patterns (i.e. customer type and
  概率加權結果、貨幣時間值及於報告日期有                             service type). The calculation reflects the probability-weighted
  關過往事件、當前狀況及未來經濟狀況預測                             outcome, the time value of money and reasonable and
  的合理及有支持的資料。關聯方的預期信貸                             supportable information that is available at the reporting date
  虧損率計算為 0.1%（二零一九年：0.1%），                        about past events, current conditions and forecasts of future
  經考慮違約率，並就前瞻性宏觀經濟數據作                             economic conditions. The expected credit loss rate for related
  出調整。                                            parties is calculated as 0.1% (2019: 0.1%) by considering the
                                                  default rates and adjusting for forward-looking macroeconomic
                                                  data.

    期損益之金融資產                    THROUGH PROFIT OR LOSS

  浮動利率的結構性銀行存款   Structured bank deposits with
                    floating interest rates                            3,847,810                     –


  結構性銀行存款產品主要為金融機構發行的             Structured bank deposits products are mainly investments in
  金融基金投資。該理財產品為保本投資，收             financial funds issued by financial institutions. Such wealth
  益率浮動且有到期日。結構性銀行存款產品             management products are principal guaranteed investments
  的合同條款於特定日期產生的現金流量並非             with floating rate of return with a maturity period. The
  僅為對本金及未償還本金利息的付款。因              contractual terms of the structured bank deposits products give
  此，結構性銀行存款產品分類為按公允價值             rise on specified dates to cash flows are not solely payments of
  計量且其變動計入當期損益。                   principal and interest on the principal amount outstanding.
                                  Accordingly, the structured bank deposits products are classified
                                  as financial assets at fair value through profit or loss.

28. 儲備                                      28. RESERVES

  本集團於本年度及先前年度的儲備款額及其                          The amounts of the Group’s reserves and the movements
  變動於財務報表的綜合權益變動表呈列。                           therein for the current and prior years are presented in the
                                               consolidated statement of changes in equity of the financial
                                               statements.

  (A) 合併儲備                                     (A) MERGER RESERVE

        本集團的合併儲備指本集團現時旗下                             The merger reserve of the Group represents the difference
        的附屬公司繳足股本總額與本集團就                             between the aggregate of the paid-up share capital of the
        共同控制下的業務合併支付的代價之                             subsidiaries now comprising the Group and the
        間的差額。                                        consideration paid by the Group for the business
                                                     combination under common control.

  (B) 法定盈餘儲備                                   (B) STATUTORY SURPLUS RESERVE

        根據中國公司法及集團公司各自之組                             In accordance with the Company Law of the PRC and the
        織章程，於中國註冊的各公司須劃撥                             respective articles of association of the group companies,
        10% 根據中國公認會計準則釐定的除                           each of the companies that is domiciled in the PRC is
        稅後溢利至法定盈餘儲備，直至達到                             required to allocate 10% of its profit after tax, as
        註冊資本的 50% 為止。轉撥至該儲備                          determined in accordance with PRC GAAP, to the
        須於向股東作出股息分派前作出。                              statutory surplus reserve until the reserve reaches 50% of
                                                     the registered capital. The transfer to this reserve must be
                                                     made before the distribution of a dividend to
                                                     shareholders.

        除清盤情況外，法定盈餘儲備為不可                             The statutory surplus reserve is non-distributable except
        分配，並受有關中國法例所載若干限                             that in the event of liquidation and, subject to certain
        制的規限，可用作抵銷累計虧損或資                             restrictions set out in the relevant PRC regulations, can be
        本化為繳足股本。                                     used to offset accumulated losses or be capitalised as
                                                     paid-up capital.

33. 金融工具的公允價值及公允價值               33. FAIR VALUE AND FAIR VALUE HIERARCHY
    層級                               OF FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS

  管理層評估認為，現金及現金等價物、列入                      Management has assessed that the fair values of cash and cash
  預付款項及其他應收款項的金融資產、貿易                      equivalents, financial assets included in prepayments and other
  應收款項、貿易應付款項與列入其他應付款                      receivables, and trade receivables, trade payables and financial
  項及應計費用的金融負債的公允價值與其賬                      liabilities included in other payables and accruals approximate to
  面值相若，主要是由於該等工具的到期時間                      their carrying amounts largely due to the short term maturities
  較短。                                      of these instruments.

  本集團財務部（由首席財務官領導）負責釐定                     The Group’s finance department headed by the chief financial
  金融工具公允價值計量的政策及程序。於各                      officer is responsible for determining the policies and procedures
  報告日期，財務經理分析金融工具價值變                       for the fair value measurement of financial instruments. At each
  動，並釐定估值使用的主要輸入數據。估值                      reporting date, the finance manager analyses the movements in
  由首席財務官審閱及批准。                             the values of financial instruments and determines the major
                                           inputs applied in the valuation. The valuation is reviewed and
                                           approved by the chief financial officer.

  結構性銀行存款，即中國內地銀行發行的理                      The fair values of structured bank deposits, which represent
  財產品，其公允價值乃於第 2 級內採用基於                    wealth management products issued by banks in Mainland
  具有類似條款及風險的工具的市場利率的現                      China, have been estimated within Level 2 using a discounted
  金流量折現估值模式估算。                             cash flow valuation model based on the market interest rates of
                                           instruments with similar terms and risks.

  裕的現金儲備及從主要金融機構取得足夠的       ensure that it maintains sufficient reserves of cash and adequate

  本集團管理其資本，以確保本集團內實體有                The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the

  平衡為股東帶來最大回報。                       maximising the return to shareholders through the optimisation
                                     of the debt and equity balance.

  本公司董事持續檢討負債與資產比率（為總                The directors of the Company review the liability-to-asset ratio,
  負債除以總資產），計及資本成本及與各類                which is total liabilities divided by total assets, on a continuous
  資本相關的風險。本集團將透過籌集新債務                basis, taking into account the cost of capital and the risks
  及贖回現有債務而平衡整體資本架構，並管                associated with each class of capital. The Group will balance its
  理負債與資產比率。本集團的整體策略於年                overall capital structure through the raising of new debts as well
  內維持不變。                             as the redemption of the existing debts, and manage the liability
                                     to asset ratio. The Group’s overall strategy remained unchanged
                                     during the year.
